- **enthropy**: Measure redundancy in a file, directory, or project. Mode 1 (default) finds textual duplicates via gzip ratios, NCD, and shingle matching. Mode 2 (--semantic) finds passages that convey the same fact in different words, using a Haiku subagent to judge candidate pairs pre-filtered by vocabulary overlap. Use when the user wants to find repeated content, near-duplicate files, "what's just being said over and over", or rephrased-but-redundant passages.
